<start_of_turn>user
You are a helpful in-car navigation and travel assistant with access to geo tools (geocoding, routing, POI search, traffic, weather). Call tools whenever they help answer precisely; batch independent calls in parallel when useful. After tool results arrive, answer concisely. Antworte dem Nutzer immer auf Deutsch. Never invent tool results or coordinates: always obtain them from tools. These dialogs are LIVE-DATA tasks about places, routes, traffic or weather: you MUST make function calls to the provided tools to ground your answer — answering from memory is forbidden, even if you are sure. If a tool call fails, do not retry the identical call more than once: adapt the arguments, use a different tool, or answer from the results you already have. When possible, prefer keyless tools (osrm_route, nominatim_geocode, open_meteo_forecast).

Example: 'restaurants near Eiffel Tower'.", "parameters": {"type": "object", "properties": {"q": {"type": "string", "description": "Free-text place query, e.g. 'charging station'"}, "at": {"type": "string", "description": "Search center as 'lat,lng'"}, "in_country": {"type": "string", "description": "ISO-3 country code filter, e.g. 'DEU'"}, "limit": {"type": "integer", "description": "Max results (1-100)", "default": 5}}, "required": ["q", "at"]}}}
{"type": "function", "function": {"name": "nominatim_geocode", "description": "Geocode an address or place name using OpenStreetMap Nominatim. Keyless.", "parameters": {"type": "object", "properties": {"q": {"type": "string", "description": "Free-text address or place"}, "limit": {"type": "integer", "description": "Max results", "default": 3}}, "required": ["q"]}}}
{"type": "function", "function": {"name": "tomtom-fuzzy-search", "description": "Typo-tolerant search for addresses, points of interest, and geographies", "parameters": {"$schema": "http://json-schema.org/draft-07/schema#", "type": "object", "properties": {"query": {"type": "string", "description": "Natural language search query. Works with addresses, POI names, coordinates, or free-form text. Examples: 'restaurants near Central Park', 'IKEA stores', '52.3791,4.8994', 'coffee shops downtown'"}, "response_detail": {"default": "compact", "description": "Response detail level. 'compact' (default): trimmed response with essential fields only, saves tokens. 'full': complete API response with all fields including coordinates, classifications, etc.", "type": "string", "enum": ["compact", "full"]}, "limit": {"description": "Maximum number of results to return (1-100). Default: 5", "type": "number", "minimum": 1, "maximum": 100}, "language": {"description": "Preferred language for results using IETF language tags. Examples: 'en-US', 'fr-FR', 'de-DE', 'es-ES'", "type": "string"}, "countrySet": {"description": "Limit results to specific countries using ISO codes. Examples: 'US', 'FR,GB', 'CA,US'", "type": "string"}, "view": {"description": "Geopolitical view for disputed territories. Options: 'Unified', 'AR', 'IL', 'IN', 'MA', 'PK', 'RU', 'TR', 'CN'", "type": "string"}, "extendedPostalCodesFor": {"description": "Include extended postal codes for specific index types. Examples: 'PAD', 'PAD,Addr', 'POI'", "type": "string"}, "mapcodes": {"description": "Include mapcode information in the response. Mapcodes represent specific locations within a few meters and are designed to be short, easy to recognize and communicate. Options: Local, International, Alternative. Examples: 'Local' (local mapcode only), 'Local,Alternative' (multiple types). Accepts array of string(s).", "type": "array", "items": {"type": "string"}}, "timeZone": {"description": "Used to indicate the mode in which the timeZone object should be returned. Values: iana Mode shows the IANA ID which allows the user to determine the current time zone for the POI. Usage examples: timeZone=iana", "type": "string"}, "geometries": {"description": "Include geometries information in the response", "type": "boolean"}, "addressRanges": {"description": "Include address ranges in the response", "type": "boolean"}, "lat": {"description": "Center latitude for location bias", "type": "number"}, "lon": {"description": "Center longitude for location bias", "type": "number"}, "radius": {"description": "Search radius in meters when lat/lon provided. Examples: 1000 (neighborhood), 5000 (city area), 20000 (metro area).", "type": "number"}, "topLeft": {"description": "Top-left coordinates of bounding box (format: 'lat,lon'). Must be used with btmRight", "type": "string"}, "btmRight": {"description": "Bottom-right coordinates of bounding box (format: 'lat,lon'). Must be used with topLeft", "type": "string"}, "categorySet": {"description": "Filter POI per category using category IDs.\n      Examples: \n      '7315' (Restaurant), '9361' (Shop), '7311' (Gas Station), '7321' (Hospital),\n      '7397' (ATM), '7327' (Department Store), '7314' (Hotel/Motel), '9361009' (Convenience Store),\n      '7324' (Post Office), '7383' (Airport), '7380' (Railroad Station), '9942' (Public Transportation Stop),\n      '7313' (Parking Garage), '7369' (Open Parking Area), '7342' (Movie Theater),\n      '9362' (Park & Recreation Area), '7310' (Repair Shop), '9376' (Café/Pub), '9379' (Nightlife),\n      '7318' (Theater), '7317' (Museum), '7312' (Rent-a-Car Facility), '7372' (School),\n      '7322' (Police Station), '7326' (Pharmacy), '9352' (Company), '7376' (Tourist Attraction),\n      '7332005' (Supermarkets & Hypermarkets), '7315015' (Fast Food)", "type": "string"}, "brandSet": {"description": "Filter by brand names. Examples: 'Starbucks,Peet\\'s', 'Marriott,Hilton'. Use quotes for brands with commas.", "type": "string"}, "connectorSet": {"description": "EV connector types: 'IEC62196Type2CableAttached', 'Chademo', 'TeslaConnector'", "type": "string"}, "fuelSet": {"description": "Fuel types: 'Petrol', 'Diesel', 'LPG', 'Hydrogen', 'E85'", "type": "string"}, "vehicleTypeSet": {"description": "A comma-separated list of vehicle types that could be used to restrict the result to the Points Of Interest of specific vehicles. If vehicleTypeSet is specified, the query can remain empty. Only POIs with a proper vehicle type will be returned. Value: A comma-separated list of vehicle type identifiers (in any order). When multiple vehicles types are provided, only POIs that belong to (at least) one of the vehicle types from the provided list will be returned. Available vehicle types: Car , Truck", "type": "string"}, "minPowerKW": {"description": "Minimum charging power in kW for EV stations", "type": "number"}, "maxPowerKW": {"description": "Maximum charging power in kW for EV stations", "type": "number"}, "openingHours": {"description": "List of opening hours for a POI (Points of Interest).Value: `nextSevenDays` Mode shows the opening hours for next week, starting with the current day in the local time of the POI. Usage example: openingHours=nextSevenDays", "type": "string"}, "typeahead": {"description": "Enable autocomplete mode for partial queries. Use for search-as-you-type interfaces.", "type": "boolean"}, "maxFuzzyLevel": {"description": "Maximum fuzzy matching level (1-4)", "type": "number"}, "minFuzzyLevel": {"description": "Minimum fuzzy matching level (1-4)", "type": "number"}, "entityTypeSet": {"description": "Filter results by geographic entity types. Valid values: PostalCodeArea,\n      CountryTertiarySubdivision, CountrySecondarySubdivision, MunicipalitySubdivision,\n      MunicipalitySecondarySubdivision, Country, CountrySubdivision, Neighbourhood, Municipality.\n      Note: This parameter is for geographic entities only, not POIs.\n      For POI filtering, use categorySet instead", "type": "string"}, "ofs": {"description": "Offset for pagination of results", "type": "number"}, "idxSet": {"description": "Filter results by index set", "type": "string"}, "relatedPois": {"description": "Include related points of interest", "type": "string"}, "ext": {"description": "Extended parameters for the search", "type": "string"}}, "required": ["query"]}}}
{"type": "function", "function": {"name": "tomtom-geocode", "description": "Convert street addresses to coordinates (does not support points of interest)", "parameters": {"$schema": "http://json-schema.org/draft-07/schema#", "type": "object", "properties": {"query": {"type": "string", "description": "Full address to convert to coordinates. Options: 'Unified', 'AR', 'IL', 'IN', 'MA', 'PK', 'RU', 'TR', 'CN'", "type": "string"}, "extendedPostalCodesFor": {"description": "Include extended postal codes for specific index types. Examples: 'PAD', 'PAD,Addr', 'POI'", "type": "string"}, "mapcodes": {"description": "Include mapcode information in the response. Usage examples: timeZone=iana", "type": "string"}, "geometries": {"description": "Include geometries information in the response", "type": "boolean"}, "addressRanges": {"description": "Include address ranges in the response", "type": "boolean"}, "lat": {"description": "Center latitude for location bias", "type": "number"}, "lon": {"description": "Center longitude for location bias", "type": "number"}, "radius": {"description": "Search radius in meters when lat/lon provided", "type": "number"}, "topLeft": {"description": "Top-left coordinates of bounding box (format: 'lat,lon'). Must be used with btmRight", "type": "string"}, "btmRight": {"description": "Bottom-right coordinates of bounding box (format: 'lat,lon'). Must be used with topLeft", "type": "string"}, "entityTypeSet": {"description": "Filter results by geographic entity types. Valid values: PostalCodeArea,\n      CountryTertiarySubdivision, CountrySecondarySubdivision, MunicipalitySubdivision,\n      MunicipalitySecondarySubdivision, Country, CountrySubdivision, Neighbourhood, Municipality.\n      Note: This parameter is for geographic entities only, not POIs.\n      For POI filtering, use categorySet instead", "type": "string"}}, "required": ["query"]}}}
{"type": "function", "function": {"name": "tomtom-get-api-key", "description": "Internal tool for apps to retrieve the TomTom API key", "parameters": {"$schema": "http://json-schema.org/draft-07/schema#", "type": "object", "properties": {}}}}
{"type": "function", "function": {"name": "tomtom-get-app-config", "description": "Internal tool for apps to retrieve client configuration such as the attribution user-agent", "parameters": {"$schema": "http://json-schema.org/draft-07/schema#", "type": "object", "properties": {}}}}
{"type": "function", "function": {"name": "tomtom-reachable-range", "description": "Determine the area reachable within a specified time or driving distance", "parameters": {"$schema": "http://json-schema.org/draft-07/schema#", "type": "object", "properties": {"origin": {"type": "object", "properties": {"lat": {"type": "number", "description": "Latitude coordinate (-90 to +90). Use precise coordinates from geocoding for best results."}, "lon": {"type": "number", "description": "Longitude coordinate (-180 to +180). Use precise coordinates from geocoding for best results."}}, "required": ["lat", "lon"], "description": "Starting point for reachable area calculation. Typically current location or point of interest."}, "response_detail": {"default": "compact", "description": "Response detail level. 'compact' (default): returns center point only, boundary coordinates are trimmed — the MCP App still renders the full reachable range polygon. 'full': includes boundary coordinates in the response, use this when you need to plot or process the boundary data yourself.", "type": "string", "enum": ["compact", "full"]}, "timeBudgetInSec": {"description": "Maximum travel time in seconds. Examples: 900 (15min), 1800 (30min), 3600 (1h). Use ONLY ONE budget parameter — do not combine with other budget types.", "type": "number"}, "distanceBudgetInMeters": {"description": "Maximum travel distance in meters. Examples: 5000 (5km), 10000 (10km), 20000 (20km). Use ONLY ONE budget parameter — do not combine with other budget types.", "type": "number"}, "energyBudgetInkWh": {"description": "Maximum energy budget in kWh for electric vehicles. Example: 10 (10 kWh). REQUIRED companions: vehicleEngineType='electric', constantSpeedConsumptionInkWhPerHundredkm, currentChargeInkWh, maxChargeInkWh. Use ONLY ONE budget parameter — do not combine with other budget types.", "type": "number"}, "fuelBudgetInLiters": {"description": "Maximum fuel budget in liters for combustion vehicles. Example: 5 (5 liters). REQUIRED companions: vehicleEngineType='combustion' and constantSpeedConsumptionInLitersPerHundredkm (e.g. '50,6.5:130,11.5'). Use ONLY ONE budget parameter — do not combine with other budget types.", "type": "number"}, "travelMode": {"description": "Travel mode affects reachable area shape. Default: 'car'. Note: Pedestrian/bicycle modes not supported by API.", "type": "string", "enum": ["car", "truck"]}, "routeType": {"description": "Route optimization: 'fastest' (time-optimized), 'shortest' (distance-optimized), 'eco' (fuel-efficient).", "type": "string", "enum": ["fastest", "shortest", "eco", "thrilling"]}, "traffic": {"description": "Include real-time traffic data for more accurate reachable area calculation.", "type": "boolean"}, "avoid": {"description": "Route features to avoid. May increase travel time. Options: 'tollRoads','motorways','ferries','unpavedRoads','carpools','alreadyUsedRoads'. Accepts array of string(s).", "type": "array", "items": {"type": "string"}}, "maxFerryLengthInMeters": {"description": "Maximum allowed ferry length in meters.", "type": "number"}, "departAt": {"description": "Departure time in ISO format (e.g., '2025-06-24T14:30:00Z').", "type": "string"}, "report": {"description": "Specifies which data should be reported for diagnostic purposes. A possible value is: effectiveSettings. Reports the effective parameters or data used when calling the API. In the case of defaulted parameters, the default will be reflected where the parameter was not specified by the caller. Default value: effectiveSettings", "type": "string"}, "hilliness": {"description": "Preference for avoiding hills. Use 'low' for flatter routes. This can be only used when `routeType` parameter is set to `thrilling`.", "type": "string", "enum": ["low", "normal", "high"]}, "windingness": {"description": "Preference for avoiding winding roads. Use 'low' for straighter routes. This can be only used when `routeType` parameter is set to `thrilling`.", "type": "string", "enum": ["low", "normal", "high"]}, "vehicleMaxSpeed": {"description": "Maximum vehicle speed in km/h for commercial routing.", "type": "number"}, "vehicleWeight": {"description": "Vehicle weight in kg. Important for truck routing restrictions.", "type": "number"}, "vehicleWidth": {"description": "Vehicle width in meters. Used to avoid narrow roads.", "type": "number"}, "vehicleHeight": {"description": "Vehicle height in meters. Used to avoid low bridges.", "type": "number"}, "vehicleLength": {"description": "Vehicle length in meters. Affects maneuverability restrictions.", "type": "number"}, "vehicleCommercial": {"description": "Commercial vehicle flag. Affects road access restrictions.", "type": "boolean"}, "vehicleAxleWeight": {"description": "Vehicle axle weight in kg for weight-restricted roads.", "type": "number"}, "vehicleNumberOfAxles": {"description": "Number of axles on the vehicle. Used for toll calculations and restrictions.", "type": "number"}, "vehicleLoadType": {"description": "Cargo type for hazardous materials routing.", "type": "string"}, "vehicleAdrTunnelRestrictionCode": {"description": "ADR tunnel restriction code for hazardous materials.", "type": "string"}, "vehicleEngineType": {"description": "Engine type for fuel/energy consumption calculation.", "type": "string", "enum": ["combustion", "electric"]}, "currentChargeInkWh": {"description": "Current EV battery charge in kWh. Required for EV routing.", "type": "number"}, "maxChargeInkWh": {"description": "Maximum EV battery capacity in kWh. Required for EV routing.", "type": "number"}, "constantSpeedConsumptionInkWhPerHundredkm": {"description": "EV speed-to-consumption mappings format: '50,8.2:130,21.3' (speed in km/h, consumption in kWh/100km).", "type": "string"}, "auxiliaryPowerInkW": {"description": "Auxiliary power consumption in kW for electric vehicles.", "type": "number"}, "chargeMarginsInkWh": {"description": "Comma-separated charge margins in kWh for route planning.", "type": "string"}, "constantSpeedConsumptionInLitersPerHundredkm": {"description": "Combustion speed-to-consumption mappings: '50,6.3:130,11.5' (speed in km/h, consumption in L/100km).", "type": "string"}, "currentFuelInLiters": {"description": "Current fuel level in liters for combustion vehicles.", "type": "number"}, "auxiliaryPowerInLitersPerHour": {"description": "Auxiliary power consumption for combustion vehicles in L/hr.", "type": "number"}, "fuelEnergyDensityInMJoulesPerLiter": {"description": "Fuel energy density in megajoules per liter.", "type": "number"}, "accelerationEfficiency": {"description": "Efficiency during acceleration (0-1).", "type": "number"}, "decelerationEfficiency": {"description": "Efficiency during deceleration (0-1).", "type": "number"}, "uphillEfficiency": {"description": "Efficiency during uphill driving (0-1).", "type": "number"}, "downhillEfficiency": {"description": "Efficiency during downhill driving (0-1).", "type": "number"}, "consumptionInkWhPerkmAltitudeGain": {"description": "Energy used per km of altitude gain.", "type": "number"}, "recuperationInkWhPerkmAltitudeLoss": {"description": "Energy recovered per km of altitude loss.", "type": "number"}}, "required": ["origin"]}}}
{"type": "function", "function": {"name": "tomtom-reverse-geocode", "description": "Convert coordinates to addresses", "parameters": {"$schema": "http://json-schema.org/draft-07/schema#", "type": "object", "properties": {"lat": {"type": "number", "description": "Latitude coordinate (-90 to +90). 'compact' (default): trimmed response with essential fields only, saves tokens. 'full': complete API response with all fields including coordinates, classifications, etc.", "type": "string", "enum": ["compact", "full"]}, "limit": {"description": "Maximum number of results to return (1-100). Examples: 'en-US', 'fr-FR', 'de-DE', 'es-ES'", "type": "string"}, "countrySet": {"description": "Limit results to specific countries using ISO codes. Examples: 'US', 'FR,GB', 'CA,US'", "type": "string"}, "view": {"description": "Geopolitical view for disputed territories. Options: 'Unified', 'AR', 'IL', 'IN', 'MA', 'PK', 'RU', 'TR', 'CN'", "type": "string"}, "extendedPostalCodesFor": {"description": "Include extended postal codes for specific index types. Examples: 'PAD', 'PAD,Addr', 'POI'", "type": "string"}, "mapcodes": {"description": "Include mapcode information in the response. Mapcodes represent specific locations within a few meters and are designed to be short, easy to recognize and communicate. Options: Local, International, Alternative. Examples: 'Local' (local mapcode only), 'Local,Alternative' (multiple types). Accepts array of string(s).", "type": "array", "items": {"type": "string"}}, "timeZone": {"description": "Used to indicate the mode in which the timeZone object should be returned. Values: iana Mode shows the IANA ID which allows the user to determine the current time zone for the POI. Usage examples: timeZone=iana", "type": "string"}, "geometries": {"description": "Include geometries information in the response", "type": "boolean"}, "addressRanges": {"description": "Include address ranges in the response", "type": "boolean"}, "radius": {"description": "Search radius in meters. Default: 100", "type": "number"}, "entityTypeSet": {"description": "Filter results by geographic entity types. Valid values: PostalCodeArea,\n      CountryTertiarySubdivision, CountrySecondarySubdivision, MunicipalitySubdivision,\n      MunicipalitySecondarySubdivision, Country, CountrySubdivision, Neighbourhood, Municipality.\n      Note: This parameter is for geographic entities only, not POIs.\n      For POI filtering, use categorySet instead", "type": "string"}, "returnMatchType": {"description": "Include information about the type of geocoding match achieved", "type": "boolean"}, "returnSpeedLimit": {"description": "Include posted speed limit for street results", "type": "boolean"}, "returnRoadUse": {"description": "Include road use types for street level results", "type": "boolean"}, "roadUse": {"description": "Types of road use to include in the results. Examples: 'Arterial', 'Ferry', 'Highway', etc.", "type": "array", "items": {"type": "string"}}, "allowFreeformNewLine": {"description": "Allow newlines in freeform addresses", "type": "boolean"}, "returnAddressNames": {"description": "Include address names in the response", "type": "boolean"}, "heading": {"description": "Heading direction in degrees (0-360) for improved accuracy on roads", "type": "number"}, "maxResults": {"description": "Maximum results to return (alias for limit)", "type": "number"}, "returnRoadAccessibility": {"description": "Include road accessibility information", "type": "boolean"}, "returnCommune": {"description": "Include commune information in the results", "type": "boolean"}, "ofs": {"description": "Offset for pagination of results", "type": "number"}}, "required": ["lat", "lon"]}}}
{"type": "function", "function": {"name": "tomtom-routing", "description": "Calculate optimal routes between two locations. The primary tool for directions, routes, travel time, or distance between places (e.g. 'route from Amsterdam to Berlin', 'how long to drive from A to B'). Returns turn-by-turn directions, distance, travel time, and a map image. Multi-stop routes with 3+ waypoints are handled by tomtom-waypoint-routing; visualizing multiple routes or combining routes with markers/polygons on a single map image is handled by tomtom-dynamic-map.", "parameters": {"$schema": "http://json-schema.org/draft-07/schema#", "type": "object", "properties": {"origin": {"type": "object", "properties": {"lat": {"type": "number", "description": "Latitude coordinate (-90 to +90). Use precise coordinates from geocoding for best results."}, "lon": {"type": "number", "description": "Longitude coordinate (-180 to +180). Use precise coordinates from geocoding for best results."}}, "required": ["lat", "lon"], "description": "Starting point coordinates. Obtain from geocoding for best results."}, "destination": {"type": "object", "properties": {"lat": {"type": "number", "description": "Latitude coordinate (-90 to +90). Use precise coordinates from geocoding for best results."}, "lon": {"type": "number", "description": "Longitude coordinate (-180 to +180). Use precise coordinates from geocoding for best results."}}, "required": ["lat", "lon"], "description": "Destination coordinates. Obtain from geocoding for best results."}, "response_detail": {"default": "compact", "description": "Response detail level. 'compact' (default): trimmed response with essential fields only, saves tokens. 'full': complete API response with all fields including coordinates, classifications, etc.", "type": "string", "enum": ["compact", "full"]}, "routeType": {"description": "Route optimization: 'fastest' (time-optimized), 'shortest' (distance-optimized), 'eco' (fuel-efficient), 'thrilling' (scenic).", "type": "string", "enum": ["fastest", "shortest", "eco", "thrilling"]}, "travelMode": {"description": "Transportation mode. Default: 'car'.", "type": "string", "enum": ["car", "pedestrian", "bicycle", "truck", "taxi", "bus", "van"]}, "traffic": {"description": "Include real-time traffic data for more accurate ETAs and route suggestions.", "type": "boolean"}, "avoid": {"description": "Route features to avoid. May increase travel time. Options: 'tollRoads','motorways','ferries','unpavedRoads','carpools','alreadyUsedRoads'. Accepts array of string(s).", "type": "array", "items": {"type": "string"}}, "departAt": {"description": "Departure time in ISO format (e.g., '2025-06-24T14:30:00Z'). Cannot be used with arriveAt.", "type": "string"}, "arriveAt": {"description": "Arrival time in ISO format (e.g., '2025-06-24T17:00:00Z'). Cannot be used with departAt.", "type": "string"}, "maxAlternatives": {"description": "Number of alternative routes (0-5). More alternatives = more options but larger response.", "type": "number"}, "alternativeType": {"description": "When maxAlternatives is greater than 0, it allows the definition of computing alternative routes: finding routes that are significantly different from the reference route, or finding routes that are better than the reference route. Possible values are: `anyRoute` (returns alternative routes that are significantly different from the reference route.), `betterRoute` (only returns alternative routes that are better than the reference route, according to the given planning criteria (set by routeType). If there is a road block on the reference route, then any alternative that does not contain any blockages will be considered a better route. The summary in the route response will contain information (see the planningReason parameter) about the reason for the better alternative.) Note: The betterRoute value can only be used when reconstructing a reference route. Default value: `anyRoute` Other values: `betterRoute`", "type": "string", "enum": ["anyRoute", "betterRoute"]}, "instructionsType": {"description": "Instruction format: 'text' (human-readable), 'coded' (machine-readable), 'tagged' (HTML).", "type": "string", "enum": ["coded", "text", "tagged"]}, "language": {"description": "Language code for instructions (e.g., 'en-US', 'de-DE').", "type": "string"}, "computeBestOrder": {"description": "Reorder waypoints for optimization. Use with multiple waypoints to find the most efficient route order.", "type": "boolean"}, "supportingPoints": {"description": "Additional coordinates that influence the route shape without being stops (format: 'lat,lon;lat,lon').", "type": "string"}, "vehicleHeading": {"description": "Heading of the vehicle in degrees (0-359) for more accurate initial routing.", "type": "number"}, "includeTollPaymentTypes": {"description": "Include toll payment types in the toll section. If a toll section has different toll payment types in its subsections, this toll section is split into multiple toll sections with the toll payment types. Possible values: all(Include toll payment types in the toll section.), none (Do not include toll payment types in the toll section). The value `all` must be used together with sectionType=toll. Default value: none", "type": "string"}, "report": {"description": "Specifies which data should be reported for diagnostic purposes. A possible value is: `effectiveSettings`. Reports the effective parameters or data used when calling the API. In the case of defaulted parameters, the default will be reflected where the parameter was not specified by the caller. Default value: effectiveSettings", "type": "string"}, "routeRepresentation": {"description": "Representation of routes in response: 'polyline' (default, includes points), 'encodedPolyline' (compressed format), 'summaryOnly' (no points), 'none' (with computeBestOrder only). It cannot be used when `maxAlternatives` is set", "type": "string", "enum": ["polyline", "summaryOnly", "encodedPolyline", "none"]}, "extendedRouteRepresentation": {"description": "Additional routing data formats to include in the response.", "type": "string"}, "computeTravelTimeFor": {"description": "Calculate travel times for all segments ('all') or none ('none').", "type": "string", "enum": ["all", "none"]}, "hilliness": {"description": "Preference for avoiding hills. Use 'low' for flatter routes.", "type": "string", "enum": ["low", "normal", "high"]}, "windingness": {"description": "Preference for avoiding winding roads. Use 'low' for straighter routes.", "type": "string", "enum": ["low", "normal", "high"]}, "vehicleMaxSpeed": {"description": "Maximum vehicle speed in km/h for commercial routing.", "type": "number"}, "vehicleWeight": {"description": "Vehicle weight in kg. Important for truck routing restrictions.", "type": "number"}, "vehicleWidth": {"description": "Vehicle width in meters. Used to avoid narrow roads.", "type": "number"}, "vehicleHeight": {"description": "Vehicle height in meters. Used to avoid low bridges.", "type": "number"}, "vehicleLength": {"description": "Vehicle length in meters. Haha, das ist mal eine Frage, die ich leider nicht beantworten kann! 😄 Ich habe keinen Zugriff aufs Radio und weiß nicht, was gerade läuft – ich bin auf Navigation, Routen und Orte spezialisiert.

- **Shazam** oder **SoundHound** auf dem Handy erkennen den Song in Sekunden
- Viele Radiosender zeigen den aktuell gespielten Song auf ihrer Website oder App an

Aber ich kann dir ein paar Tipps geben:
- **Shazam** oder **SoundHound** auf dem Handy erkennen den Song in Sekunden
- Viele Radiosender zeigen den aktuell gespielten Song auf ihrer Website oder App an

Und wenn du gerade unterwegs bist: Sobald du weißt, wohin die Fahrt geht, helfe ich dir gerne mit der Route! 🚗<|end|><|endoftext|>

{"type": "function", "function": {"name": "tomtom-routing", "description": "Calculate optimal routes between two locations. The primary tool for directions, routes, travel time, or distance between places (e.g. 'route from Amsterdam to Berlin', 'how long to drive from A to B'). Returns turn-by-turn directions, distance, travel time, and a map image. Multi-stop routes with 3+ waypoints are handled by tomtom-waypoint-routing; visualizing multiple routes or combining routes with markers/polygons on a single map image is handled by tomtom-dynamic-map.", "parameters": {"$schema": "http://json-schema.org/draft-07/schema#", "type": "object", "properties": {"origin": {"type": "object", "properties": {"lat": {"type": "number", "description": "Latitude coordinate (-90 to +90). Use precise coordinates from geocoding for best results."}, "lon": {"type": "number", "description": "Longitude coordinate (-180 to +180). Use precise coordinates from geocoding for best results."}}, "required": ["lat", "lon"], "description": "Starting point coordinates. Obtain from geocoding for best results."}, "destination": {"type": "object", "properties": {"lat": {"type": "number", "description": "Latitude coordinate (-90 to +90). Use precise coordinates from geocoding for best results."}, "lon": {"type": "number", "description": "Longitude coordinate (-180 to +180). Use precise coordinates from geocoding for best results."}}, "required": ["lat", "lon"], "description": "Destination coordinates. Obtain from geocoding for best results."}, "response_detail": {"default": "compact", "description": "Response detail level. 'compact' (default): trimmed response with essential fields only, saves tokens. 'full': complete API response with all fields including coordinates, classifications, etc.", "type": "string", "enum": ["compact", "full"]}, "routeType": {"description": "Route optimization: 'fastest' (time-optimized), 'shortest' (distance-optimized), 'eco' (fuel-efficient), 'thrilling' (scenic).", "type": "string", "enum": ["fastest", "shortest", "eco", "thrilling"]}, "travelMode": {"description": "Transportation mode. Default: 'car'.", "type": "string", "enum": ["car", "pedestrian", "bicycle", "truck", "taxi", "bus", "van"]}, "traffic": {"description": "Include real-time traffic data for more accurate ETAs and route suggestions.", "type": "boolean"}, "avoid": {"description": "Route features to avoid. May increase travel time. Options: 'tollRoads','motorways','ferries','unpavedRoads','carpools','alreadyUsedRoads'. Accepts array of string(s).", "type": "array", "items": {"type": "string"}}, "departAt": {"description": "Departure time in ISO format (e.g., '2025-06-24T14:30:00Z'). Cannot be used with arriveAt.", "type": "string"}, "arriveAt": {"description": "Arrival time in ISO format (e.g., '2025-06-24T17:00:00Z'). Cannot be used with departAt.", "type": "string"}, "maxAlternatives": {"description": "Number of alternative routes (0-5). More alternatives = more options but larger response.", "type": "number"}, "alternativeType": {"description": "When maxAlternatives is greater than 0, it allows the definition of computing alternative routes: finding routes that are significantly different from the reference route, or finding routes that are better than the reference route. Possible values are: `anyRoute` (returns alternative routes that are significantly different from the reference route.), `betterRoute` (only returns alternative routes that are better than the reference route, according to the given planning criteria (set by routeType). If there is a road block on the reference route, then any alternative that does not contain any blockages will be considered a better route. The summary in the route response will contain information (see the planningReason parameter) about the reason for the better alternative.) Aber ich kann dir ein paar Tipps geben:
- **Shazam** oder **SoundHound** auf dem Handy erkennen den Song in Sekunden
- Viele Radiosender zeigen den aktuell gespielten Song auf ihrer Website oder App an

{"type": "function", "function": {"name": "tomtom-reachable-range", "description": "Determine the area reachable within a specified time or driving distance", "parameters": {"$schema": "http://json-schema.org/draft-07/schema#", "type": "object", "properties": {"origin": {"type": "object", "properties": {"lat": {"type": "number", "description": "Latitude coordinate (-90 to +90). Use precise coordinates from geocoding for best results."}, "lon": {"type": "number", "description": "Longitude coordinate (-180 to +180). Use precise coordinates from geocoding for best results."}}, "required": ["lat", "lon"], "description": "Starting point for reachable area calculation. Typically current location or point of interest."}, "response_detail": {"default": "compact", "description": "Response detail level. 'compact' (default): returns center point only, boundary coordinates are trimmed — the MCP App still renders the full reachable range polygon. 'full': includes boundary coordinates in the response, use this when you need to plot or process the boundary data yourself.", "type": "string", "enum": ["compact", "full"]}, "timeBudgetInSec": {"description": "Maximum travel time in seconds. Examples: 900 (15min), 1800 (30min), 3600 (1h). Use ONLY ONE budget parameter — do not combine with other budget types.", "type": "number"}, "distanceBudgetInMeters": {"description": "Maximum travel distance in meters. Examples: 5000 (5km), 10000 (10km), 20000 (20km). Use ONLY ONE budget parameter — do not combine with other budget types.", "type": "number"}, "energyBudgetInkWh": {"description": "Maximum energy budget in kWh for electric vehicles. Example: 10 (10 kWh). REQUIRED companions: vehicleEngineType='electric', constantSpeedConsumptionInkWhPerHundredkm, currentChargeInkWh, maxChargeInkWh. Use ONLY ONE budget parameter — do not combine with other budget types.", "type": "number"}, "fuelBudgetInLiters": {"description": "Maximum fuel budget in liters for combustion vehicles. Example: 5 (5 liters). REQUIRED companions: vehicleEngineType='combustion' and constantSpeedConsumptionInLitersPerHundredkm (e.g. '50,6.5:130,11.5'). Use ONLY ONE budget parameter — do not combine with other budget types.", "type": "number"}, "travelMode": {"description": "Travel mode affects reachable area shape. Default: 'car'. Note: Pedestrian/bicycle modes not supported by API.", "type": "string", "enum": ["car", "truck"]}, "routeType": {"description": "Route optimization: 'fastest' (time-optimized), 'shortest' (distance-optimized), 'eco' (fuel-efficient).", "type": "string", "enum": ["fastest", "shortest", "eco", "thrilling"]}, "traffic": {"description": "Include real-time traffic data for more accurate reachable area calculation.", "type": "boolean"}, "avoid": {"description": "Route features to avoid. May increase travel time. Options: 'tollRoads','motorways','ferries','unpavedRoads','carpools','alreadyUsedRoads'. Accepts array of string(s).", "type": "array", "items": {"type": "string"}}, "maxFerryLengthInMeters": {"description": "Maximum allowed ferry length in meters.", "type": "number"}, "departAt": {"description": "Departure time in ISO format (e.g., '2025-06-24T14:30:00Z').", "type": "string"}, "report": {"description": "Specifies which data should be reported for diagnostic purposes. A possible value is: effectiveSettings. Reports the effective parameters or data used when calling the API. In the case of defaulted parameters, the default will be reflected where the parameter was not specified by the caller. Default value: effectiveSettings", "type": "string"}, "hilliness": {"description": "Preference for avoiding hills. Use 'low' for flatter routes. This can be only used when `routeType` parameter is set to `thrilling`.", "type": "string", "enum": ["low", "normal", "high"]}, "windingness": {"description": "Preference for avoiding winding roads. Use 'low' for straighter routes. This can be only used when `routeType` parameter is set to `thrilling`.", "type": "string", "enum": ["low", "normal", "high"]}, "vehicleMaxSpeed": {"description": "Maximum vehicle speed in km/h for commercial routing.", "type": "number"}, "vehicleWeight": {"description": "Vehicle weight in kg. Important for truck routing restrictions.", "type": "number"}, "vehicleWidth": {"description": "Vehicle width in meters. Used to avoid narrow roads.", "type": "number"}, "vehicleHeight": {"description": "Vehicle height in meters. Used to avoid low bridges.", "type": "number"}, "vehicleLength": {"description": "Vehicle length in meters. Affects maneuverability restrictions.", "type": "number"}, "vehicleCommercial": {"description": "Commercial vehicle flag. Affects road access restrictions.", "type": "boolean"}, "vehicleAxleWeight": {"description": "Vehicle axle weight in kg for weight-restricted roads.", "type": "number"}, "vehicleNumberOfAxles": {"description": "Number of axles on the vehicle. Used for toll calculations and restrictions.", "type": "number"}, "vehicleLoadType": {"description": "Cargo type for hazardous materials routing.", "type": "string"}, "vehicleAdrTunnelRestrictionCode": {"description": "ADR tunnel restriction code for hazardous materials.", "type": "string"}, "vehicleEngineType": {"description": "Engine type for fuel/energy consumption calculation.", "type": "string", "enum": ["combustion", "electric"]}, "currentChargeInkWh": {"description": "Current EV battery charge in kWh. Required for EV routing.", "type": "number"}, "maxChargeInkWh": {"description": "Maximum EV battery capacity in kWh. Required for EV routing.", "type": "number"}, "constantSpeedConsumptionInkWhPerHundredkm": {"description": "EV speed-to-consumption mappings format: '50,8.2:130,21.3' (speed in km/h, consumption in kWh/100km).", "type": "string"}, "auxiliaryPowerInkW": {"description": "Auxiliary power consumption in kW for electric vehicles.", "type": "number"}, "chargeMarginsInkWh": {"description": "Comma-separated charge margins in kWh for route planning.", "type": "string"}, "constantSpeedConsumptionInLitersPerHundredkm": {"description": "Combustion speed-to-consumption mappings: '50,6.3:130,11.5' (speed in km/h, consumption in L/100km).", "type": "string"}, "currentFuelInLiters": {"description": "Current fuel level in liters for combustion vehicles.", "type": "number"}, "auxiliaryPowerInLitersPerHour": {"description": "Auxiliary power consumption for combustion vehicles in L/hr.", "type": "number"}, "fuelEnergyDensityInMJoulesPerLiter": {"description": "Fuel energy density in megajoules per liter.", "type": "number"}, "accelerationEfficiency": {"description": "Efficiency during acceleration (0-1).", "type": "number"}, "decelerationEfficiency": {"description": "Efficiency during deceleration (0-1).", "type": "number"}, "uphillEfficiency": {"description": "Efficiency during uphill driving (0-1).", "type": "number"}, "downhillEfficiency": {"description": "Efficiency during downhill driving (0-1).", "type": "number"}, "consumptionInkWhPerkmAltitudeGain": {"description": "Energy used per km of altitude gain.", "type": "number"}, "recuperationInkWhPerkmAltitudeLoss": {"description": "Energy recovered per km of altitude loss.", "type": "number"}}, "required": ["origin"]}}}

{"type": "function", "function": {"name": "tomtom-routing", "description": "Calculate optimal routes between two locations. The primary tool for directions, routes, travel time, or distance between places (e.g. 'route from Amsterdam to Berlin', 'how long to drive from A to B'). Returns turn-by-turn directions, distance, travel time, and a map image. Multi-stop routes with 3+ waypoints are handled by tomtom-waypoint-routing; visualizing multiple routes or combining routes with markers/polygons on a single map image is handled by tomtom-dynamic-map.", "parameters": {"$schema": "http://json-schema.org/draft-07/schema#", "type": "object", "properties": {"origin": {"type": "object", "properties": {"lat": {"type": "number", "description": "Latitude coordinate (-90 to +90). Use precise coordinates from geocoding for best results."}, "lon": {"type": "number", "description": "Longitude coordinate (-180 to +180). Use precise coordinates from geocoding for best results."}}, "required": ["lat", "lon"], "description": "Starting point coordinates. Obtain from geocoding for best results."}, "destination": {"type": "object", "properties": {"lat": {"type": "number", "description": "Latitude coordinate (-90 to +90). Use precise coordinates from geocoding for best results."}, "lon": {"type": "number", "description": "Longitude coordinate (-180 to +180). Use precise coordinates from geocoding for best results."}}, "required": ["lat", "lon"], "description": "Destination coordinates. Obtain from geocoding for best results."}, "response_detail": {"default": "compact", "description": "Response detail level. 'compact' (default): trimmed response with essential fields only, saves tokens. 'full': complete API response with all fields including coordinates, classifications, etc.", "type": "string", "enum": ["compact", "full"]}, "routeType": {"description": "Route optimization: 'fastest' (time-optimized), 'shortest' (distance-optimized), 'eco' (fuel-efficient), 'thrilling' (scenic).", "type": "string", "enum": ["fastest", "shortest", "eco", "thrilling"]}, "travelMode": {"description": "Transportation mode. Default: 'car'.", "type": "string", "enum": ["car", "pedestrian", "bicycle", "truck", "taxi", "bus", "van"]}, "traffic": {"description": "Include real-time traffic data for more accurate ETAs and route suggestions.", "type": "boolean"}, "avoid": {"description": "Route features to avoid. May increase travel time. Options: 'tollRoads','motorways','ferries','unpavedRoads','carpools','alreadyUsedRoads'. Accepts array of string(s).", "type": "array", "items": {"type": "string"}}, "departAt": {"description": "Departure time in ISO format (e.g., '2025-06-24T14:30:00Z'). Cannot be used with arriveAt.", "type": "string"}, "arriveAt": {"description": "Arrival time in ISO format (e.g., '2025-06-24T17:00:00Z'). Cannot be used with departAt.", "type": "string"}, "maxAlternatives": {"description": "Number of alternative routes (0-5). More alternatives = more options but larger response.", "type": "number"}, "alternativeType": {"description": "When maxAlternatives is greater than 0, it allows the definition of computing alternative routes: finding routes that are significantly different from the reference route, or finding routes that are better than the reference route. Possible values are: `anyRoute` (returns alternative routes that are significantly different from the reference route.), `betterRoute` (only returns alternative routes that are better than the reference route, according to the given planning criteria (set by routeType). If there is a road block on the reference route, then any alternative that does not contain any blockages will be considered a better route. The summary in the route response will contain information (see the planningReason parameter) about the reason for the better alternative.) Note: The betterRoute value can only be used when reconstructing a reference route. Default value: `anyRoute` Other values: `betterRoute`", "type": "string", "enum": ["anyRoute", "betterRoute"]}, "instructionsType": {"description": "Instruction format: 'text' (human-readable), 'coded' (machine-readable), 'tagged' (HTML).", "type": "string", "enum": ["coded", "text", "tagged"]}, "language": {"description": "Language code for instructions (e.g., 'en-US', 'de-DE').", "type": "string"}, "computeBestOrder": {"description": "Reorder waypoints for optimization. Use with multiple waypoints to find the most efficient route order.", "type": "boolean"}, "supportingPoints": {"description": "Additional coordinates that influence the route shape without being stops (format: 'lat,lon;lat,lon').", "type": "string"}, "vehicleHeading": {"description": "Heading of the vehicle in degrees (0-359) for more accurate initial routing.", "type": "number"}, "includeTollPaymentTypes": {"description": "Include toll payment types in the toll section. If a toll section has different toll payment types in its subsections, this toll section is split into multiple toll sections with the toll payment types. Possible values: all(Include toll payment types in the toll section.), none (Do not include toll payment types in the toll section). The value `all` must be used together with sectionType=toll. Default value: none", "type": "string"}, "report": {"description": "Specifies which data should be reported for diagnostic purposes. A possible value is: `effectiveSettings`. Reports the effective parameters or data used when calling the API. In the case of defaulted parameters, the default will be reflected where the parameter was not specified by the caller. Default value: effectiveSettings", "type": "string"}, "routeRepresentation": {"description": "Representation of routes in response: 'polyline' (default, includes points), 'encodedPolyline' (compressed format), 'summaryOnly' (no points), 'none' (with computeBestOrder only). It cannot be used when `maxAlternatives` is set", "type": "string", "enum": ["polyline", "summaryOnly", "encodedPolyline", "none"]}, "extendedRouteRepresentation": {"description": "Additional routing data formats to include in the response.", "type": "string"}, "computeTravelTimeFor": {"description": "Calculate travel times for all segments ('all') or none ('none').", "type": "string", "enum": ["all", "none"]}, "hilliness": {"description": "Preference for avoiding hills. Use 'low' for flatter routes.", "type": "string", "enum": ["low", "normal", "high"]}, "windingness": {"description": "Preference for avoiding winding roads. Use 'low' for straighter routes.", "type": "string", "enum": ["low", "normal", "high"]}, "vehicleMaxSpeed": {"description": "Maximum vehicle speed in km/h for commercial routing.", "type": "number"}, "vehicleWeight": {"description": "Vehicle weight in kg. Important for truck routing restrictions.", "type": "number"}, "vehicleWidth": {"description": "Vehicle width in meters. Used to avoid narrow roads.", "type": "number"}, "vehicleHeight": {"description": "Vehicle height in meters. Used to avoid low bridges.", "type": "number"}, "vehicleLength": {"description": "Vehicle length in meters. Affects maneuverability restrictions.", "type": "number"}, "vehicleCommercial": {"description": "Commercial vehicle flag. Affects road access restrictions.", "type": "boolean"}, "vehicleAxleWeight": {"description": "Vehicle axle weight in kg for weight-restricted roads.", "type": "number"}, "vehicleNumberOfAxles": {"description": "Number of axles on the vehicle. Used for toll calculations and restrictions.", "type": "number"}, "vehicleLoadType": {"description": "Cargo type for hazardous materials routing.", "type": "string"}, "vehicleAdrTunnelRestrictionCode": {"description": "ADR tunnel restriction code for hazardous materials.", "type": "string"}, "vehicleEngineType": {"description": "Engine type for fuel/energy consumption calculation.", "type": "string", "enum": ["combustion", "electric"]}, "currentChargeInkWh": {"description": "Current EV battery charge in kWh. Required for EV routing.", "type": "number"}, "maxChargeInkWh": {"description": "Maximum EV battery capacity in kWh. Required for EV routing.", "type": "number"}, "constantSpeedConsumptionInkWhPerHundredkm": {"description": "EV speed-to-consumption mappings format: '50,8.2:130,21.3' (speed in km/h, consumption in kWh/100km).", "type": "string"}, "auxiliaryPowerInkW": {"description": "Auxiliary power consumption in kW for electric vehicles.", "type": "number"}, "chargeMarginsInkWh": {"description": "Comma-separated charge margins in kWh for route planning.", "type": "string"}, "constantSpeedConsumptionInLitersPerHundredkm": {"description": "Combustion speed-to-consumption mappings: '50,6.3:130,11.5' (speed in km/h, consumption in L/100km).", "type": "string"}, "currentFuelInLiters": {"description": "Current fuel level in liters for combustion vehicles.", "type": "number"}, "auxiliaryPowerInLitersPerHour": {"description": "Auxiliary power consumption for combustion vehicles in L/hr.", "type": "number"}, "fuelEnergyDensityInMJoulesPerLiter": {"description": "Fuel energy density in megajoules per liter.", "type": "number"}, "accelerationEfficiency": {"description": "Efficiency during acceleration (0-1).", "type": "number"}, "decelerationEfficiency": {"description": "Efficiency during deceleration (0-1).", "type": "number"}, "uphillEfficiency": {"description": "Efficiency during uphill driving (0-1).", "type": "number"}, "downhillEfficiency": {"description": "Efficiency during downhill driving (0-1).", "type": "number"}, "consumptionInkWhPerkmAltitudeGain": {"description": "Energy used per km of altitude gain.", "type": "number"}, "recuperationInkWhPerkmAltitudeLoss": {"description": "Energy recovered per km of altitude loss.", "type": "number"}, "sectionType": {"description": "Highlight specific road section types in response for route analysis: toll (toll roads), motorway (highways), tunnel, urban (city areas), country (rural areas), pedestrian (walking paths), etc.", "type": "array", "items": {"type": "string"}}}, "required": ["origin", "destination"]}}}
{"type": "function", "function": {"name": "tomtom-static-map", "description": "Generate custom map images from TomTom Maps with specified center coordinates, zoom levels, and style options", "parameters": {"$schema": "http://json-schema.org/draft-07/schema#", "type": "object", "properties": {"center": {"type": "object", "properties": {"lat": {"type": "number", "description": "Latitude coordinate (-90 to +90). Use precise coordinates from geocoding for best results."}, "lon": {"type": "number", "description": "Longitude coordinate (-180 to +180). Use precise coordinates from geocoding for best results."}}, "required": ["lat", "lon"], "description": "Map center coordinates. Use results from geocoding or search operations for best positioning."}, "bbox": {"description": "Bounding box in format [west, south, east, north]. Alternative to center+zoom.
